    "    typeof window.requestIdleCallback === 'function'\r\n" +
    '      ? window.requestIdleCallback(() => fn(), { timeout: 2000 })\r\n' +
    '      : window.setTimeout(fn, 200);\r\n' +

    '    const loader = rest.shift();\r\n' +
    '    if (!loader) return;\r\n' +
    '    loader()\r\n' +
    '      .catch(() => undefined)\r\n' +
    '      .finally(() => idle(next));\r\n' +
    '  };\r\n' +

    '}\r\n' +

    '    </div>\r\n' +
